INDIAN AIRLINES EMPLOYEES 1 PROVIDENT 
FUND NEW DELHI 

New Delhi \ the 20 th April 19/1 

No. P.F. 11/ 2. — In exercise of the powers conferred 
by section 45 of the Air Corporations Act, 1953 (27 of 
1953), and with the previous approval of tlie Central 
Government, the Indian Airlines hereby makes the 
following regulations further to amend the Indian Air- 
lines Employees' Provident Fund Regulations, 1955, 
namely : — 

1* These regulation;, may he called the Indian Air- 
lines Employees Provident Fund (Amendment) Regula- 
tions, 1971. 


2. They shall conic into force with effect from -fhe 
28th July 1969. 

3. In the Indian Airlines Employees' Provident Fund 
Regulations, 1955 (hereinafter referired to as the said 
Regulations), in regulation 12, 

(1) in sub-regulation (2), 

for the proviso under Explanation , the follow- 
ing proviso sh|Jl be substituted , namely : — 

‘‘Provided further, that notwithstanding any- 
thing contained in clause (f) of regulation 2 
of these Regulations or in any Service Rules 
o’r Regulations of the Corporation for the time 
being in force, the Corporation may, in its 
discretion, permit a person employed on con- 
tract or otherwise for a limited period or in 
the circumstances contemplated in regulation 
12- A to become a member of the Fund on 
such terms and conditions as the Corporation 
may decide in each individual case.”; 

(2) after sub-regulation (2), the following sub- 
regulation snail be inserted , namely 

“(3) A Pilot or a Flight Engineer of the 
Corporation opting for annuity under the 
rules of the Corporation on becoming per- 
manently medically unfit for Hying duties 
may be permitted to become a member of 
the Fund from the first of the month follow- 
ing the month in which he is re-einployed 
in the Corporation as a Pilot or a Flight 
Engineer, as the case may be, upon regain- 
ing medical fitness. Such an employee shall 
be entitled to the Corporation's contribution 
and interest thereon but the same will be 
paid to him in* accordance with regulation 
20 : Provided that for the purpose of clause 
(i) of sub-regulation (2) of regulation 20, 
the length of membership of such a member 
prior to his opting for annuity upon be- 
coming medically unfit for flying duties will 
also be taken into account.” 

4. For regulation 1 2- A of the said Regulations, the 
following regulation shall be substituted, namely ; — 

"12* A Pt 'ocedure on employment of a person who 
was previously a subscriber to any Provident Fund 
of the Government, or a Body Corporate , owned or 
controlled by the Government : 

Where a person employed in the Corporation was 
previously a subscriber to any Provident Fund of the 
Government or a Body Corporate, owned or controlled 
by the Government, the transfer of his provident fund 
accumulations from the Government Department or the 
body corporate, namely, his subscriptions as also the 
contributions of the previous employer, if any, together 
with interest thereon, shall be accepted as an initial 
deposit in his account in I he Fund., Such a person will 
be permitted to become a member from the date of 
commencement of his employment in the Corporation 
or from the elate of his {resignation from the service of 
the Government or body corporate owned or controlled 
by the Government takes effect, whichever is later. The 
Corporation’s contribution will also be admissible to 
such an employee from the date Ills membership takes 
effect : 

Provided that after the acceptance of the transfer, the 
provident fund account of the employee shall be govern- 
ed by the regulations of the Fund. 1 ’ 

S. K, CHATTERJEE 
Secretary
